## `AGO-2825-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Headspace-Gas Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Food Contact Sanitary 316L Stainless Steel Surface Roughness Ra
- **Intro:** Headspace-Gas Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Food Contact Sanitary 316L Stainless Steel Surface Roughness Ra — Verify Steady-State Conditions: Temperature: 85°C ± 1°C; Pressure: 3.0 bar ± 0.05 bar; Flow rate: 120 L/min ± 2% (i.e., 117.6 to 122.4 L/min).
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 85°C ± 1°C; Pressure: 3.0 bar ± 0.05 bar; Flow rate: 120 L/min ± 2% (i.e., 117.6 to 122.4 L/min).
- **Standards:** `ISO 4287:1997`, `ISO 4288:1996`, `ASME B46.1-2019 - Surface Texture (Surface Roughness, Waviness, `, `ASTM A240/A240M-20 - Standard Specification for Chromium and Chr`
